[A second account](../guides/second-account.md) is the guide: find, add, import, change, make default and remove a profile, step by step. Most examples below come from [`examples/acct-second-account`](../../examples/acct-second-account/README.md), which runs ([ubgo/agx](https://github.com/ubgo/agx/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/profile.md))
- **profile**: Build your profile once (offer, proof, voice, clients, memberships) so leads fit you and notes sound like you. ([WeipengZhuo/lead-finder](https://github.com/WeipengZhuo/lead-finder/blob/HEAD/plugins/lead-finder/commands/profile.md))
- **profile**: `pinax profile` manages backend/API connection profile aliases. Profiles store connection metadata and secret references, not raw credentials. ([yeisme/pinax](https://github.com/yeisme/pinax/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/profile.md))
- **profile**: Inspect or toggle the two-tier plugin profile (ALWAYS vs ON-DEMAND) — list, lean, full, enable/disable one. ([yotamleo/Himmel](https://github.com/yotamleo/Himmel/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/profile.md))
- **profile-and-explore**: Profile and explore a dataset before anyone models it: shape/types/missingness/cardinality/distributions, documented cleaning, leakage candidates, and hypotheses with uncertainty. ([mcorbett51090/RavenClaude](https://github.com/mcorbett51090/RavenClaude/blob/HEAD/plugins/data-science-research/commands/profile-and-explore.md) · ★9)
- **profile-api**: Profile an API endpoint to identify performance bottlenecks and optimization opportunities. ([rohitg00/awesome-claude-code-toolkit](https://github.com/rohitg00/awesome-claude-code-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/performance-monitor/commands/profile-api.md) · ★2.8k · 12 copies)
- **profile-app**: Profile App for React Performance Optimization ([rdimascio/react-marketplace](https://github.com/rdimascio/react-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/react-performance/.claude/commands/profile-app.md) · ★2)
- **profile-audit**: Audit and rewrite a LinkedIn profile against verified facts ([rubicon/ai-skills](https://github.com/rubicon/ai-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/salience/commands/profile-audit.md) · ★1)
- **profile-automation**: Wraps [`agents/automation-transaction-profiler/AGENT.md`](../agents/automation-transaction-profiler/AGENT.md). ([PranavNagrecha/AwesomeSalesforceSkills](https://github.com/PranavNagrecha/AwesomeSalesforceSkills/blob/HEAD/commands/profile-automation.md) · ★26)
- **profile-automation**: Show which Salesforce automation runs for an object operation, in what order, and where recursion or DML amplification exists. Read-only. Fixture-first via get_automation_inventory. ([PranavNagrecha/AwesomeSalesforceSkills](https://github.com/PranavNagrecha/AwesomeSalesforceSkills/blob/HEAD/integrations/cursor/commands/profile-automation.md) · ★26)
- **profile-build**: Arma el borrador de un perfil de cargo desde la ocupación de su estándar, con cada requisito atado a una tarea, para que una persona lo apruebe ([josenanez/criterio](https://github.com/josenanez/criterio/blob/HEAD/plugins/criterio-roles/commands/profile-build.md) · ★1)
- **profile-catalog**: Read-only. Asks the open Tekla model which profile names it accepts and, on request, lists the library profiles with their shape family and dimensions. It exists so a caller can find out BEFORE a `bake-scene` which profiles the model lacks, and suggest a match for each, instead of discovering it as a failed batch. ([aware-aeco/aware](https://github.com/aware-aeco/aware/blob/HEAD/20-agents/aeco/engineering/tekla/commands/profile-catalog.md) · ★7)
- **profile-change**: Switch to a plugin profile — updates active plugins to match the target profile ([dmedina-dev/dev-forge](https://github.com/dmedina-dev/dev-forge/blob/HEAD/plugins/forge-profiles/commands/profile-change.md) · ★1)
- **profile-check**: Cada perfil de cargo contra la ocupación de su estándar: sin ocupación, lo que no recoge, requisitos sin tarea, estudios que no corresponden y la revisión vencida ([josenanez/criterio](https://github.com/josenanez/criterio/blob/HEAD/plugins/criterio-roles/commands/profile-check.md) · ★1)
- **profile-clone**: Lifecycle: single ([aware-aeco/aware](https://github.com/aware-aeco/aware/blob/HEAD/20-agents/aeco/architecture/revit-2025/commands/profile-clone.md) · ★7 · 3 copies)
- **profile-code**: Profile application code to identify performance bottlenecks in frontend and backend systems. ([Agentient/vibekit](https://github.com/Agentient/vibekit/blob/HEAD/skills/agentient-performance/commands/profile-code.md) · ★2)
- **profile-corpus**: Regenerate builder-internal evidence + card dials from a corpus ([solanabr/writer-style-skill](https://github.com/solanabr/writer-style-skill/blob/HEAD/commands/profile-corpus.md) · ★2)

- **profile-create**: Register a new Tekla profile prefix as an **alias of a section Tekla already loads**, by adding one row to the environment's `profitab.inp` (the folder named by `XS_PROFDB`). The new prefix then measures and takes sizes exactly like the shape it aliases. ([aware-aeco/aware](https://github.com/aware-aeco/aware/blob/HEAD/20-agents/aeco/engineering/tekla/commands/profile-create.md) · ★7)

- **profile-cu**: Profile compute unit usage per instruction in a Solana program ([solanabr/solana-vault-standard](https://github.com/solanabr/solana-vault-standard/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/profile-cu.md) · ★24 · 9 copies)
